Using almost the same argument above, we also have the similar results:

\displaystyle\eqref{essst21}\lesssim\eta^{3/4}_{3}2^{i-l}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u_{H}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G^{i}_{\alpha},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})},

\displaystyle\eqref{essst24}\lesssim\eta^{3/4}_{3}2^{i-l}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u_{H}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G^{i}_{\alpha},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}.

So, by the definition of

,

(7.48)

Next we treat
\|F^{L}_{2}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}
. By (
7.4
),
\|F^{L}_{2}\|^{2}_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\lesssim\sum\limits_{J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha}\neq\emptyset}\|F^{L}_{2}\|^{2}_{U_{\Delta}^{2}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G_{\alpha}^{i}),L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}.

Using Lemma
7.1
, on every interval
J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha}
,
g_{l^{\prime}}\in U_{\Delta}^{6}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha}),L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\|F^{L}_{2}\|_{U^{2}_{\Delta}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha}),L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup\limits_{\|g_{l^{\prime}}\|_{U^{6}_{\Delta}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha}),L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}=1}\int_{J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha}}\big\langle g_{l^{\prime}},P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq i-10}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq i-10}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{L}

(7.49)

\displaystyle\hskip 25.83325pt\times P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq i-10}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq i-10}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}.

\displaystyle\eqref{qweewq}\lesssim\big\|\|P_{m}^{x_{1}}g_{l^{\prime}}\|_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L_{t,x_{1}}^{6}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha})\times\mathbb{R})}\big\|\|u_{\leq i-10}\|_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|^{2}_{L_{t,x_{1}}^{6}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha})\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 25.83325pt\times\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\|u_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L_{t,x_{1}}^{2}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha})\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 25.83325pt\lesssim\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\|u_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L_{t,x_{1}}^{2}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha})\times\mathbb{R})}.

So,

(7.50)

\displaystyle\|F^{L}_{2}\|^{2}_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G^{i}_{\alpha}\neq\emptyset}\|F^{L}_{2}\|^{2}_{U_{\Delta}^{2}((J_{l^{\prime}}\cap G_{\alpha}^{i}),L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}.

),

Thus

(7.51)

\displaystyle\|F^{L}_{2}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\lesssim 2^{(i-l)/2}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{U^{2}_{\Delta}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|u\|_{X(G_{\alpha}^{i},L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}.

Next we estimate (
7.34
). Without loss of generality, we assume that the
\Pi_{j_{2}}u,\Pi_{j_{3}}u,\Pi_{j_{4}}u
are replaced by

respectively.

Let

be the left endpiont of subinterval

, on every

, we further decompose

\displaystyle\int_{a_{\alpha}^{i}}^{t}e^{i(t-\tau)\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)d\tau

as

(7.52)

\displaystyle\int_{a^{i}_{\alpha}}^{\gamma^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}}e^{i(t-\tau)\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)d\tau

(7.53)

\displaystyle+\int_{\gamma^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}}^{t}e^{i(t-\tau)\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m-3}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)d\tau

and denote

\displaystyle\int_{a^{i}_{\alpha}}^{t}e^{i(t-\tau)\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)d\tau

\displaystyle=F^{m_{3}}_{1}+F^{m_{3}}_{2},

and

on every

.

First we treat
\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq m_{3}\leq m_{2}\\
\leq j-10\end{subarray}}\|F^{m_{3}}_{1}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}
. By the embedding

,

\displaystyle\|F^{m_{3}}_{1}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F^{m_{3}}_{1}\|_{V_{\Delta}^{1}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\subset G^{j}_{k}}\bigg\|\int_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}}e^{-i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq i-10}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}

(7.54)

\displaystyle\times P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\Pi_{j_{5}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\big)d\tau\bigg\|_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}.

Notice that for any

, there exists

,
\|f^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}=1
and has Fourier transform supported on

such that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}}e^{-i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}

\displaystyle\hskip 25.83325pt\times P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)d\tau\bigg\|_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle=\int_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}}\big\langle f_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}},e^{-i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}

\displaystyle\hskip 25.83325pt\times P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}d\tau.

Denote
\widetilde{F}_{m_{3}}(t,x_{1},x_{2})=\sum\limits_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\subset G^{i}_{\alpha}}\chi_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}}(t)f^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}(x_{1},x_{2})
, then by Hölder’s inequality

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{G^{i}_{\alpha}}e^{-i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}

\displaystyle\hskip 25.83325pt\times P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)d\tau\bigg\|_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{G^{i}_{\alpha}}\big\langle\widetilde{F}_{m_{3}}(\tau),e^{-i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\Pi_{j}\big(P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}\Pi_{j_{1}}uP^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{2}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}

\displaystyle\hskip 25.83325pt\times P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u\big)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\|e^{i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\widetilde{F}_{m_{3}}(\tau)\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\times\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{1}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{i}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\|e^{i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\widetilde{F}_{m_{3}}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\|P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}

(7.55)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\times\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L^{2}_{x_{2}}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{1}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{i}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}.

), Proposition

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\subset G^{i}_{\alpha}}\big\|\|e^{i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\widetilde{F}_{m_{3}}(\tau)\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\times\big\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum\limits_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\subset G^{i}_{\alpha}}\big\|\|e^{i\tau\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m}\widetilde{F}_{m_{3}}(\tau)\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\times\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\times\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}2^{(m_{3}-l)/2}\sum\limits_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\subset G^{i}_{\alpha}}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|e^{it\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}}f^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\times\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}2^{(m_{3}-l)/2}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

(7.56)

So,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq m_{3}\leq m_{2}\\
\leq j-10\end{subarray}}\eqref{estttttt1}\lesssim\eta_{2}\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq m_{3}\leq m_{2}\\

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\times\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{i}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}2^{(i-l)/2}\big(\sum\limits_{m_{3}\leq j-10}2^{m_{3}-i}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u\|^{2}_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\big(\sum\limits_{m_{3}\leq j-10}\big(\sum\limits_{m_{3}\leq m_{2}\leq j-10}\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{i}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}\big)^{2}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}\|u\|_{X(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

(7.57)

\displaystyle\times\big(\sum\limits_{m_{3}\leq j-10}\big(\sum\limits_{m_{3}\leq m_{2}\leq j-10}\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{i}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}\big)^{2}\big)^{1/2}.

For fixed
m_{2}
and

, using Hölder’s inequality, bilinear estimate (

\displaystyle\times\big(\sum\limits_{m_{3}\leq j-10}\big(\sum\limits_{m_{3}\leq m_{2}\leq j-10}2^{(m_{3}-l)/2}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),m_{3}}u\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\big)^{2}\big)^{1/2}

that is,

(7.59)

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq m_{3}\leq m_{2}\\
\leq j-10\end{subarray}}\|F^{m_{3}}_{1}\|_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\lesssim\eta_{2}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}u\|^{2}_{U^{2}_{\Delta}(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|u\|_{X(G_{\alpha}^{i},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}.

Next we treat the final term

\leq j-10\end{subarray}}\big(\sum\limits_{G^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\subset G^{i}_{\alpha}}\|F^{m_{3}}_{2}\|^{2}_{U_{\Delta}^{2}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\big)^{1/2}.

Noticing by Lemma

\displaystyle\hskip 86.11084pt\times P_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}^{x_{1}}\Pi_{j_{3}}u_{H}P_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}^{x_{1}}\overline{\Pi_{j_{4}}u_{H}}P_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}^{x_{1}}\Pi_{j_{5}}u)\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x_{1}}L_{x_{2}}^{2}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R})}d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 55.97205pt\lesssim\sup\limits_{\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{U^{12/5}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}}))}=1}\big\|\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}}\times\mathbb{R})}

(7.60)

\displaystyle\hskip 86.11084pt\times\big\|\|P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}^{x_{1}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}}\times\mathbb{R})}.

\sup\limits_{\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{U^{12/5}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}=1}\big\|\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{U^{12/5}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}=1}\big\|\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\lesssim\sup\limits_{\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{U^{12/5}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}=1}\big\|\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{12/5}_{t,x_{1}}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 43.05542pt\times\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{12}_{t,x_{1}}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\lesssim 2^{({m_{3}}-l)/4}\sup\limits_{\|g^{m_{3}}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\|_{U^{12/5}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}=1}\|g^{m_{3}}_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}\|_{U^{12/5}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|u\|_{U^{12/5}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|u\|_{X(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\lesssim 2^{({m_{3}}-l)/4}\|P_{\xi(\tau),m_{3}}^{x_{1}}u\|_{U^{2}_{\Delta}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|u\|_{X(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

(7.62)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.22217pt\lesssim 2^{({m_{3}}-l)/4}\|u\|^{2}_{X(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}.

On the other hand, by bilinear estimate (

), compactness condition (

), Proposition

and Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\big\|\|P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}^{x_{1}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G^{i}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\|P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}^{x_{1}}u\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L^{2}_{t,x_{1}}(G_{{\alpha}^{\prime}}^{m_{3}}\times\mathbb{R})}\big\|\|P^{x_{1}}_{\xi(\tau),\leq m_{3}}u_{H}\|_{L_{x_{2}}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\big\|_{L_{t,x_{1}}^{\infty}(G^{i}_{\alpha}\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}2^{{m_{3}}/2}2^{-l/2}\|P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}^{x_{1}}u\|_{U^{2}_{\Delta}(G^{i}_{\alpha},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|P_{\xi(\tau),m_{2}}^{x_{1}}u\|_{U^{2}_{\Delta}(G^{i}_{\alpha},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}

(7.63)

\displaystyle\lesssim\eta_{2}2^{({m_{3}}-m_{2})/2}2^{(i-l)/2}\|P_{\xi(G^{i}_{\alpha}),l}^{x_{1}}u\|_{U^{2}_{\Delta}(G^{i}_{\alpha},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}\|u\|_{X(G^{i}_{\alpha},L_{x_{1}}^{2}L_{x_{2}}^{2})}.

By (
